## 24 November 2026 | Integrated design and source review

Reviewed current published code and approved Master Reconstruction Blueprint (resource concurrency, local-first/cloud consent, W01/W07/E-02); read resource policy, API heavy entrypoints and training capacity, workspace lease, model loader and export response, Rust launch and shutdown, browser API preflight, server/desktop entrypoints, provider/settings and build scripts, resource measurement evidence. Primary technical references in spec. Identified actual pre-allocation, cross-process, synchronous launch, no-secret, settings publication, local-to-cloud fallback and unlocked build issues. Selected one authoritative admission coordinator and preferred existing owned pipe bridge, bounded asynchronous launch, explicit dev capability, shared owned settings writer/consent correction, exact target build input locks. Compatibility gates remain explicit; exact locked version public docs unavailable for Tauri shell, no actual bridge runtime proof or complete route inventory yet. No code/test/native/model/installer/owner-data operation performed; no fresh approval. B V2 SUCCESS remains historical and consumed. C0/5, source 10/17 (58.8%), P4 14/20 (70%), G2 BLOCKED, P5 unauthorized, P8 installer frozen. Preparation journal `a12328650a42528a18a462ac0197640725e45309`; publication marker C-SPECIFICATION-20261124-publish. Next C1 exact route/lifetime inventory and bounded implementation, no broad research loop.

## 24 November | C1 strict reply boundary and standalone Rust wire source published

Operation C1-RESOURCE-ADMISSION-20261124-host-wire, prepared at `df3beab`, published [`b642614`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/b6426147dccd94747924abbd5ba4c3be6133d7b8). Python rejects pipelined complete/partial second request before dispatch; requires exact trusted write/flush acknowledgement before next read; retains release lease through acknowledgement; faults on wrong/duplicate ack or lost write. Versioned STOP reply renamed ADMISSION_STOPPED, not exit. Rust standalone dependency-free Wire mirrors generation, sequence, one pending request, grant correlation, strict bounded reply framing and fail-closed fault state; five Rust unit tests staged but NOT compiled/run, not registered in lib.rs. Seven added Python test cases (29 host expected); existing split test updated for sequential replies rather than accepting pipelining. Reviewed all 82 workflow triggers, only P1 source workflow matched these paths; [exact CI #45460620652](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/45460620652) IN_PROGRESS at observation. Actual shell 2.6.0 framing/log isolation, owned transport timeout, native grant/exit and unified registration outstanding. No native/model/installer/owner operation. C0/5, source 10/17, P4 14/20 unchanged.

## 25 November | C4 baseline research and proposed implementation plan (not implementation or closure)

Baseline [`7db2f60`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2); C3 source closed, no outstanding source CI, C4 0/1; C 3/5, overall 13/17, official P4 14/20. Reviewed governing C-D06/07, sections 8–10, Master Reconstruction Blueprint (privacy/provider boundary, W05, E-07, offline behavior, settings and credentials), and current source at exact baseline. Blueprint requires cloud OFF by default, explicit provider/payload consent, no automatic cloud after local failure, no raw rows under existing policy, OS-protected keys, preserved local tools. No new approval or product decision; proposal implements existing contract. No native execution, owner data, actual provider call, model operation, migration or dependency acquisition.

### Source baseline and risk register

| Area / priority | Confirmed current implementation or open verification issue |
| --- | --- |
| Critical: implicit egress | [`backend/ai_provider.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/ai_provider.py#L430) legacy respond uses resolved saved preferences, truthy flags, Hybrid local failure -> cloud, selected provider followed by other providers, Gemini alternate model retry, non-strict urlopen. Owned cloud implementation already has exact true flags, explicit provider, no fanout, no continuation, strict opener and consent/context rechecks; two paths diverge. |
| Critical: invisible calls | [`backend/main.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/main.py#L50) legacy lifespan automatically probes connected providers; verify-connected legacy fanout/cooldown. [`frontend/src/App.tsx`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/frontend/src/App.tsx#L244) navigation/mount verification; [`frontend/src/AIWorkspace.tsx`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/frontend/src/AIWorkspace.tsx#L24) Save key automatically tests connection even if cloud consent is off on legacy path; auto-insights effect may dispatch saved cloud provider (including in owned composition). Legacy test_connection only checks key, not consent. |
| High: validation | [`backend/ai_provider.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/ai_provider.py#L112) unbounded config read and merge, ignored unknown fields, no strict validation on read, shallow default copies; invalid root/types and DPAPI failures can break config_status/health. String truthiness can authorize legacy cloud. [`backend/main.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/main.py#L1063) request models use coercive bool/defaults; strict bounded bodies and secret-bearing validation error redaction require direct tests. Do not assume framework errors omit submitted values. |
| High: settings publication | [`backend/ai_provider.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/ai_provider.py#L160) fixed ai_settings.tmp written before its identity is checked, no explicit fsync, target identity not retained from start and no post-commit readback. [`backend/resource_policy.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/resource_policy.py#L57) uses exclusive stage+flush/fsync, but target identity and postcheck missing, unbounded read, permissive schema_version comparison. [`backend/workspace_boundary.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/workspace_boundary.py#L688) path checks are lease-scoped observed identity, no-op for unleased legacy/fixture callers; not hostile swap proof. Cleanup must not unlink unknown-identity stage. |
| High: key store | [`backend/ai_provider.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/ai_provider.py#L65) Windows DPAPI current-user with UI forbidden; existing base64 ciphertext representation retained. Reads unbounded, loose decoding/schema; exclusive stage lacks explicit fsync and postcheck, errors can propagate. _get_key uses environment before stored keys: removing stored key may leave ambient key active. Distinguish key presence vs tested connection vs consent; removing locally does not revoke upstream service key. Non-Windows memory-only preserved. |
| High: payload | [`backend/ai_context.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/ai_context.py#L41) existing bounded structural builder, evaluation field allowlist, local individual prediction section. Legacy cloud removes only top-level prediction and can include continuation tail; owned excludes previous generated text but still copies other arbitrary context keys and generic default=str conversion. Field/target names, typed questions, min/max and small cohorts can expose sensitive facts; [`backend/analytics.py`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/blob/7db2f60e7daa4e4f975822503b4e78008f4977e2/backend/analytics.py#L380) safe investigation summary omits category values, but verify counts and bounded nested fields. Need cloud-specific positive projection, actual encoded byte limits, no claims anonymous. |
| High: concurrency | Owned consent revision already increments before updates; strict dispatch checks before open, response publication check. Legacy route and probe bypass them. Frontend insight cache/pending keys contain project/version/provider but not consent generation; auto and previous selection may publish stale result; consent revocation not equivalent to cancellation of running blocking network I/O. Legacy context lacks equivalent final version/archive freshness guard. |
| High: provider subrouting | Current OpenRouter model is openrouter/free, which is a router rather than fixed downstream model; provider-side automatic failover is distinct from PilotODIN-side failover. Verify and disclose downstream scope, do not promise single ultimate recipient from hostname alone. No provider availability/model substitution assumed. |
| Compatibility | Existing tests/test_multi_ai.py and tests/test_hybrid_local_ai.py explicitly expect legacy failover, cloud-after-local failure and non-strict wire mocks. Those expectations must be deliberately superseded by blueprint-aligned negatives and positive explicit provider tests, not deleted silently. Existing owned tests in tests/test_c1_product_integration.py cover selection/consent/revocation/no-probe/dispatch. Keep original A/B/C1–C3 regression assertions. |

### Research references (documentation, not provider execution)

- [Microsoft CryptProtectData](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/dpapi/nf-dpapi-cryptprotectdata) and [CryptUnprotectData](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/dpapi/nf-dpapi-cryptunprotectdata): retain user-bound DPAPI, UI forbidden, LocalFree; not protection against arbitrary code acting as same user. Bound input/output and error handling; zero temporary mutable buffers where practical, no claim Python strings can be reliably wiped.
- [Python 3.11 os](https://docs.python.org/3.11/library/os.html), [JSON](https://docs.python.org/3.11/library/json.html), [urllib.request](https://docs.python.org/3.11/library/urllib.request.html): flush/fsync, same-filesystem replace, strict bounded JSON duplicate-key rejection with object_pairs_hook, explicit no-proxy/no-redirect transport. Atomic namespace replacement is not full Windows power-loss or hostile filesystem proof. [Windows FlushFileBuffers](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/fileapi/nf-fileapi-flushfilebuffers) informs later native durability verification; do not introduce native calls without source compatibility and distinct evidence.
- [OWASP SSRF prevention](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Server_Side_Request_Forgery_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html), [Secrets Management](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Secrets_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html), [Logging](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Logging_Cheat_Sheet.html): explicit allowlist, no redirect, least privilege, revocation, no credential/payload logging.
- [OpenRouter free router](https://openrouter.ai/openrouter/free), [provider routing](https://openrouter.ai/docs/guides/routing/provider-selection), [routing explanation](https://openrouter.ai/blog/insights/model-routing/): router can select model and underlying provider. Restrict model/recipient set and failover where supported; if exact scope cannot be established, fail closed for project disclosure until reviewed. Do not silently select paid model, broaden consent or claim zero retention. Any changed provider disclosure/scope requiring product choice is reviewed before shipping.

### Proposed architecture (existing stack, no migration or extra service)

C3 authenticated route -> strict small settings/intent validation -> Python authoritative settings/credential snapshot and consent generation -> explicit request classifier (local deterministic, local model, manual cloud insight, manual minimal provider test) -> C1/C2 existing admission and context staging -> cloud positive-field projection and bounded serialized payload -> single strict selected-provider transport -> generation/project/version/stop recheck -> verified result or local fallback. No separate Rust consent authority, no independent legacy cloud router, no native activation switch. React shows provider/destination/payload and local fallback status; remembered provider selection is preference, not automatic dispatch authority. Missing/invalid intent never authorizes cloud. Backend cannot prove an actual human click from arbitrary authenticated HTTP; C3 trust and explicit UI action plus server-side request contract are scoped protections, not hostile same-user proof.

Settings core: one process RLock shared across resource/AI/keys; existing owned workspace lease/pins required in production; explicit disposable adapter for tests (no implicit runtime bypass). Keep exactly resource_preferences.json (schema_version 1/mode), ai_settings.json (existing flat field schema, recognized older missing fields use defaults), ai_secrets.dpapi (current base64 encrypted provider map); no new persistent consent field, database changes or project backup inclusion. Strict exact bool/int/enums, provider/order/workspace map/model name/bounds; reject duplicate keys, non-finite, wrong root, extra/future fields for unsafe rewrite, unsupported version. Missing file is distinct from invalid/unreadable; invalid returns safe effective local-only view with fixed warning, original unchanged and replacement refused pending explicit safe recovery. Proposed initial file bounds 4 KiB resource, 16 KiB AI JSON, 64 KiB encoded ciphertext/16 KiB decrypted JSON, subject to exact legacy fixture and DPAPI capacity check before coding. Bounded read on original verified regular handle, compare lstat/fstat and identity/content before/after; no raw path or values in diagnostics. Invalid key map blocks cloud without blocking health/local analytics.

Owned replacement helper: fixed target enum, validate admitted existing parent, observe target absent or exact regular identity/content, create exclusive same-directory stage (retain existing .secrets-/ .resource-policy- convention; narrowly admit new random AI-settings stage, do not delete historical fixed .tmp), write bounded serialized bytes; encrypted bytes only for secret target; check write count, flush/fsync, verify original handle and stage path, verify unchanged target/absence and lease/pins immediately before replace, postcheck identity/size/hash and parse/decrypt as appropriate. Cleanup only exact stage owned by current operation; no sweep, no backup/restore, never delete unknown identity. Distinguish confirmed save, known not committed, commit uncertain; don't claim old file unchanged after possible successful replace; no retry ambiguous mutation. Existing valid prefs/ciphertext survive injected precommit failures; postcommit uncertainty may preserve new bytes and blocks further cloud until reconciliation. POSIX/Windows observed identity and fsync limitations explicit, native crash/power-loss/physical race evidence deferred D. Use narrow existing storage/workspace checks rather than reimplement A/B or broad filesystem API.

Credential design: retain DPAPI and session-only non-Windows, no key in config/API response/URL/query/prompt/log/cache/localStorage/backup; bound base64 and decrypted map with exact provider keys and existing validated length, encryption failure leaves prior ciphertext; do not automatically migrate/delete. Environment keys are currently supported even after Remove: propose production use stored credential only, explicit isolated dev/test env adapter with clearly labelled provenance (verify compatibility before implementation); no hidden fallback to another key following removal. If approved dev env credential remains, Remove must not claim it is gone and must inhibit cloud for that provider until explicit new selection/credential authorization. Provider health cache bound to current generation, never claim key saved = connected. Saving/rotating/removing key is local-only; connection test separately explicit, fixed minimal handshake with existing owned consent requirement (both saved flags, permitted mode, selected provider). If pre-consent credential test desired, separate reviewed disclosure/authorization needed, not automatic.

Routing: one common backend policy for legacy and owned; select destination from actual explicit manual request, not saved provider order or automatic workspace insight. Auto is local-only; local mode forbids external even with saved flags; disabled/missing/malformed or pending revocation -> no dispatch. Cloud-only without explicit destination -> deterministic/local defer, not automatic cloud. Exact named provider only, no Gemini alternate retry or cross-provider retry; simplest one attempt. Explicit provider test no dataset context and no auto save/startup/navigation probing; verify-connected API preserved as passive cached status. Provider error -> deterministic verified result, truthful reason; no silent local-worker wake after cloud failure. Keep local continuation; refuse cloud continuation history, require fresh explicit question. Strict fixed HTTPS URL/model registry, no proxies/redirects, reject unexpected response status/shape/size, close on every branch, fixed safe categories; use approved current endpoints only. No request from provider-generated instructions.

Payload: separate positive nested schema for existing task, version, numeric counts/stats, bounded column names/quality categories, safe feature screening, approved aggregate model metrics and investigation summaries, typed question disclosed. No arbitrary dict copying, default=str, file paths, IDs not needed by provider, dataset rows, categories, individual prediction, previous answer, arbitrary report narrative, keys. Detect/bound small group/exact values and sensitive names; propose suppressing small-group stats (threshold to be reviewed with fixture/blueprint), not claiming anonymization or automatic PII detection. Preserve local detailed output; actual serialized UTF-8 user payload <= existing 11000 byte budget (and bounded whole wire body/system/response); keep existing 256000 response-byte cap, output bounds, 3–45 s settings timeout, test connection deadline consistent with consent, bounded concurrent cloud dispatch and no unbounded retry/queue. Verify per-socket timeout vs total deadline; do not release a slot while blocking worker still active.

Revocation: shared generation increment/inhibit before save/key mutation, immutable short-lived request snapshot (provider, operation, generation, key identity, project/version and payload class; never expose secret). Atomic admission/cancel state under short lock; recheck latest flags/key/target and context at last practical dispatch seam and before result, release lock before network. Reject pending before dispatch, discard late results and update health only if same generation. Mark dispatched conservatively before entering network; do not claim already submitted bytes recalled or perfect first-byte cancellation. Inhibit on failed/uncertain revoke, distinguish process-only stop from persisted change and warn restart state may still hold old flags; explicit action still required after restart. Frontend abort where safe, invalidate pending/cache on consent/credential generation and context change, discard late results, do not delete saved reports/history. Preserve navigation, deterministic offline and C1/C2 admission/release invariants. Cloud consent decision and data/project state not trusted from model response.

Proposed bounds and actual runtime fixture compatibility confirmed before source patch. |
| B: unified cloud boundary | Reuse owned strict implementation as basis, converge legacy and owned; remove hidden probes/legacy fanout, explicit intent selection, positive payload projection, generation/cancel/health semantics, exact selected-provider transport. Handle OpenRouter disclosure before enabling project data; keep provider IDs/schema. Fake wire and barrier tests for all paths; no actual provider request. |
| C: frontend contract and integrated verification | Update AIWorkspace/AssistantWorkspace/App/API status/preflight and cached/pending requests. Auto local-only; Save key no test; explicit visible provider/payload action; safe failure and uncertain-save UI. Preserve settings valid values, local navigation/model/analytics; no silent new consent field or UI product scope. Both compositions, full API/browser fixtures, no real network/model. |
| D: C4 closure evidence | Finish caller/egress and storage ownership matrix, exact CI terminal receipts, retained historical failures, no unsafe legacy fallback, prove synthetic failure/revocation/payload negatives. Then C4 source +1 only; C5 next. D package (native/physical) not activated by this batch. |

Cloud zero-egress on startup, mount, settings/key save, verify-connected, auto, local selection, disabled/invalid consent, absent/revoked key, invalid destination, canceled/stale context; explicit selected positive fixture, mock all 3 providers, 3xx/proxy refusal, no model/provider fallback, bounded sanitized payload including Unicode/sensitive/rare-group/continuation, malformed/oversize response, 401/429/503/timeout; barrier revocation before dispatch, during I/O, before result, failed persistence/late health, duplicate/busy; frontend context change and cached response fences. Existing historical tests asserting contrary behavior explicitly revised with documented reason and new negative expectation, not silently removed. Reuse P1 isolated backend/frontend/lifecycle/real HTTP, P2/P3 affected regression, P4 consolidated exact scope based on actual trigger review; no workflow edits or restricted CI in planning. Python 3.11/Pydantic 2 existing ranges, exact installed versions checked from CI before relying on framework features. Synthetic DPAPI injection only; actual Windows account-bound encryption/power-loss and physical egress/8 GB verification remain D/fresh authority.
